# RANKED — the door's first reading (SAME-2 brief D part 2)

Tree **7e93c087b**. Reading: `reading.hxd.json` from the run `runs/hxd` (150 places a size, six sizes), measured with `scripts/sameness-census.mjs :: measureSlots` (the door's slot key and attribution); no new render. Classes are the register's (`?` = the rule's guess).

## SUMMARY LINES

- **A SENTENCE REPEATED INSIDE ONE PLACE** (the owner's stop condition; first): places printing one sentence twice or more within one surface family, per size — thorp 150/150 (STORY 150; STORY at ONE position 0), hamlet 150/150 (STORY 150; STORY at ONE position 9), village 150/150 (STORY 150; STORY at ONE position 9), town 150/150 (STORY 150; STORY at ONE position 43), city 150/150 (STORY 150; STORY at ONE position 141), metropolis 150/150 (STORY 150; STORY at ONE position 150). STORY = a STATE or NARRATIVE position; the CATALOGUE/LABEL repeats (a house's name twice) are counted apart. "At one position" is two cards or ties in one list printing one wording.
- Rows over the bar (commonest sentence on more than 15% of ALL places of a size): **438** slots; per size: thorp 247, hamlet 260, village 261, town 339, city 381, metropolis 383.
- COVERED BY NOTHING YET: **360** of the 438 (of them, 51 carry only an unsure `?` lane beside it); rows with any `?` lane: 60.
- Many-a-place (meet is SEEN at some size): **318** slots; over the 30% bar per size: thorp 71, hamlet 80, village 83, town 84, city 137, metropolis 170.
- By class (`?` folded in): LABEL 229, CATALOGUE 67, STATE 85, NARRATIVE 57; COVERED BY NOTHING YET by class: LABEL 220, CATALOGUE 64, STATE 45, NARRATIVE 31. LABEL and CATALOGUE are the same by design (the page's furniture, a house's own definition); the top five by weight are all furniture, so the STATE and NARRATIVE top five follow them.
- THE CARD'S SECRET (the web card's secret slot): sentences on 15% or more of cities **178**, of metropolises **263** (PAGE-8: 169 and 259); "divine visions" 106/150 metropolises; SEEN thorp 5.5%, hamlet 5.6%, village 16.2%, town 23.3%, city 26.3%, metropolis 30.8%.

## A SENTENCE REPEATED INSIDE ONE PLACE (the chair, 20:14)

Measured by `scripts/sameness-census.mjs :: repeatsInPlace` over every place of the run: a sentence printed twice or more inside ONE place within one surface FAMILY (the web dossier is one family, each PDF form one, the session sheet one), at any positions, the same position included. Sentences are MASKED (each place's names to `#`, digits to `0`), so one wording printed twice with other names in it counts: the frame repeats. SIDE: STORY where a position's register class is STATE or NARRATIVE (the fault), FURNITURE where every position is CATALOGUE or LABEL (a house's name or a caption twice is not the fault), UNROWED where no position has a register row (fewer than 5 places print it). Every repeated sentence, per size, is in `REPEATS.tsv` beside this file.
